Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the solar_physics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 469 and the convergence rate is O(436/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 469 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the solar_physics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 436. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
